BIBLIOGRAPHY. 
108 
JANAPANI 
Sonatchi  aro  Kalsinni  dakmesokaniko  1937-38  bilsirangoni  intalan  Achik  asongni  salgro 
Jolo  chongmotan  Penchimari,  Rongchipatpara,  Moskuli,  Derek  (Assam)  aro  gipin  biaprangoba 
dakmesoke  mesokaha.  Haida,  ua  sonatchi  aro  Kalsinni  golpoko  Bengali  oikorchi,  Pa  Upendro 
Ch.  Momin  sechengaha  ine  nikaha,  1930  bilsirangon  matchotna  nangaha. 
Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  gimin  chugimik  uamangni  kam  aro  kattarangni  gimin  dakmesokani 
aganbewalo  seaha.  Iandakgipa  Kalsin  aro  sonatchini  dakmesokani  chu  soksrange  knalo  an  chingni 
A chik  bisarangna  u i ma  sirike  ra  rikchina  donangenga  maina  iandakdipa  dakmesokanirang 
jatni  gisepo  dao  gimaangengaha. 
Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  gimin  asel  chongmot  banoni  aro  mai  mai  ong  achim  u'ina  aro  masina 
skanirang  bangan  dongam  gimin,  anga  ia  ki  tapko  seanio  mana  dipct  dakmesokaniko 
dakdilchenggiparango,  meapa  chagilchamrango  singsandie  aro  sandirikote  jeko  man  a talatcnga. 
Achikrang  rokomari  dakmesokanirangko  dakmesokna,  chrok  mesana,  git  rigngna,  ringringna 
aro  daksningna  changgiparang  onga.  Indiba,  iarang  plakko  daksninge  dakmesokna  chol 
ong  pagijanio  dongjripna  nangaha. 
Sonatchi  aro  Kalsinni  dakmesokaniko  skanggipa  dakdilchenggiparangoni  Pa  Nogen  Sangma 
(sagi)  Rongchipatpara  songom  ong  a.  Uni  ja  mano  Moskuli  songo  Pa  Ramesor  K.  sangma,  1940 
jolrangoni  abachengskaha.  Ja  manode  ka  sine  ka  sine  gipin  biaprangonaba,  Jemangan 
dakmesokaniko  inesokna  gisik  nanga,  ra  roroe  gipangaha  aro  club  ba  dolrangkoba  a-bachengaha. 
Indiba  ia  plak  jatni  dakmesokanirang  jattangni  didianiko  man  gijanichi.  Cinema,  T.  V.  ba  andakgipa 
rokomari  nika  nianirang  A chik  a songonaba  gipbaanichi,  1 980  jolrangoni  intal  ka  sine  ka  sine 
bon  angalia.  Ia  dakmesokanirangara  jatni  mingsa  nambegipa  dakbewalko  dakmesokani  ong'achim. 
Haida,  ia  ki  tap  saoba  jatni  gisepo  iandakgipa  dakmesokanirangko  ripingangna  sikgiparangna 
dakchakani  ong  naba  donga  ine  chanchiesa  chu  gimik  dakmesokaniko  seaha.  Iandakgipa 
dakmesokanirangko  jatni  gisepo  ripingangna  nanga.  uni  gimin  jc  manderangan  ia  dakmesokaniko 
poraigen  nama  gimikko  skic  ra  e namgijako  gale  A chik  pagitcham  chagitchamrangni  namgipa 
chanchibewalani  katta  aro  kamko  ripingna  knalona  jotton  ka  na. 
Sonatchi  aro  Kalsinni  gimin  plak  katta  bichongrangko  agan  talate  on*e  angko 
dakchakgiparangna  jemangkon  iano  sena  chol  ong  jaha  plakkon  mitela 
Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  dakmesokaniko  anga  an  tangan  songni  me  apa,  me  chikma  aro 
dakmesokaniko  dakdilgiparango  sing  sandie  seaha.  Uamangni  gimin  bang  an,  ma  sina  aro  talna 
skanirang  bang  a indiba  anga  ia  manderangni  nangnikanirangko  ia  damesokani  ki  tapo  chu  sokatna 
man  jaha.  Redin  Momin,  Sonatchi  aro  Kalsinni  gimin  golponi  bimango  seaha  indiba  dakmesokani

bimango  dongkuja.  Uni  a'bachengo  dakdilchenggipa  dakmesokani  seanirangko  sandie  nie  aro 
chong amot  ong  aniko  sing  jike  da  o la  dakmesokani  bimango  nakatatenga. 
Uni  gimin  ia  kitapko  poraie  nie  masigiparang,  saoba  lalbate  masiode  aro  uarangko  u'ialpaode 
anga  uarangna  kusi  ong'begen.  Ua  namdapatani  kattarangko  changgipin  nipiltaie  chapa  ka'atanio 
on'dapalaniko  on  a kusi  ong'begen. 
Dated  Shillong. 
The  4th  June,  1996. 
Julius  L.R.  Marak. 
KALSIN  ARO  SONATCHI 
KATTA  BIMA 
* * * * 
Kalsin  aro  Sonatchi-mangni  katta  bichong  chongmot  mai  ongachim,  darangba  sandirikote 
nia  dongkuja.  Kalsin  aro  sonatchini  golpo  saoba  mandeni  chongmot  janggi  tangani  ongama  ba 
ong-jachimma  namedake  masina  manja.  Saoba  mandeni  chong  mol  janggi  tangani  onggenchimode, 
uamangara  bano  songdong  a chara  chiga  chiringa  namedake  sandic  nina.  Pa  Redin  Mrenda 
Momin-ni  Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  gimin  golponi  bimango  see  donanggipako  1972  bilsio  chappa 
ka  atgiminko  nikna  man  gcn.  Redin  Momin,  a bachengani  agananio  indi  ine  janapaha. 
"dingtangdingtang  jolrango  Sonatchini  golporangko  agananirang  gnang.  Indiba  ia  ki  tapo 
seanirangsa  a bachenggipa  aro  chongmotgipa  katta  onga.  Neng  bee  sanditaiesa  ia  chu'gimik 
seaniko  chirsokatna  manaha".  Redin  Momin.  Sonatchi  aro  Kalsinni  golpoko,  Chong*mot  mandeni 
janggi  tangani  chi  ma,  ba  badiaba  golpoonikosa  Achikni  bewal  aro  dakbewalrangko  rae  seachimma, 
lalatanga  dongja. 
Pa  Redin  mominni  golponi  bimango  kosako  janapgimin  katlarango  pangchake  see 
donanggipani  gimin,  maiva  dipet  che  em  cheem  u’igipa  ma’sikuengipa  manderango  sandiroroe 
talatangan  baksana  banoni  ong  baa  katta  ja  pangkotalatangtaienga  jedakodeong*a  ba  ong  gijakoba 
iri  ma  siriktaichina  aro  chckriklaichina  dakmesokani  bimango  see  donanglaienga.  Da*o  ia  chasongo, 
A chik  skia  poraia,  bida  buti  dongbatokengahani  gimin  rokomari  jatni  gisepo  jerikotanirang  aro 
chong  mot  ong  a obostako  ma  sina  jotton  ka  a.  Manderangni  ku  birangrangoniko  knaman’enga 
gita,  Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  golpoko  Reddin  Momin  seja  indiba  sakgipinni  seakosa  namdapate 
chappa  ka  ataha.  Mittam  ku  bisringrang  gam  enga,  Redin  Momin  an  tangan  Bengali  golponikosa 
pe  skaenba  uko  jatni  golpo  ong  jaoba,  A chikni  bewal  aro  dakbewalrangona  ra  gate  segatskara. 
Undake  manderangni  agan  ku  riting-rangoniko  knana  man  e,  anga  an  tangan  kakketko  ma  sina 
ske  jeko  man  engkua  sandirikote  nitaie,  Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  golpoko  Dakmesokani  bimango, 
chong  mot  ong  a ba  ong  gijako  u i ma  sigipa  me  aparango  sing  jike  nakatataienga.  A bachengo 
Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  golpo  Dakmesokani  bimangosa  ong  achim.

Redin  Mrenda  Monin,  Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  golpoko  scanggiminko  Pa  Grimchi  C.  Marak 
(Chaware)  1972  bilsio  skanggipa  chappa  ka  ataha.  Da  o Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  golpoko  A*chik

bongagipa  chappa  ka  attaigipa  1987  bilsio  ong  a.

patpara,  Goalpara,  Assam,  songrango  Kalsin  aro  sonatchini  dakmesokaniko  dakdilchengaha.

manja  indiba  namen  ja  man  chaka  ine  chanchiate  niatna  man  gen.  Reddin  Momin  songo  noko 
dongmittingoba  (Amjonga  songo)  toromko  skiprakna  songrangchi  reroroa  ine  aganna.  Uni  uandake 
reroromitting  sal  somoirango  ba  Turao  dongmittingo,  Upcndro  Ch.  Mominni  Bengali  oikorchi 
poetry  dake  segipa  Kalsin  aro  Sonatchini  golpo  krtapko  man  e ripinge  doime,  dao  chappa 
ka  aha.  Redin  M.  Momin,  antangan  bidana  aro  toromna  gisik  nanggipa  A’chik  panterangoni 
saksa  ongani  gimin  ia  kitap  Achik  bi  sarangna  maiba  skidapani  ongnaba  donga  ine  nike,  uni 
poetryni  bimango  segipako  setaie  aro  namdapate  donangaha. 
Uni  uandake  skia  poraianiko,  gisikni  senganiko  aro  janggi  tanganio  skie  ra  ani  cholrangko 
ripinge  donangna  gisik  nanganiko  kamchi  mesokaha.  Uni  gisik  nange,  ripinge  sarie  aro 
chanchibewale  see  donangjagenchim  ong  ode  iandakgipa  gamchatbegipa  seanirang  indin  a kin 
cha'e  gimaaingnokchim.  Reddin  M.  Momin  aro  Sonatchi  Kalsinni  golpoko  an  tang 
sechengjagenchim  ongoba,  jatna  ka  sae  apsan  katta  bichongo  pangchake  seangaha  je  kamnan 
mitelna  kraa.  Reddin  Momin  bonchotao  ia  katlarangko  seangaha, 
"Branggipa  bisaoba,  mande  saksa  ong  jok, 
Kakket  aro  bebeo  ua  plakkon  amtokjok. 
Bebe  dakgipanade  lsolba  patia, 
Dukko  chakgipanasa  Isol  Ka'sachaka". 
A chik  jatni  sea  jotanirang  maikai  mikkang  sal  somoirangchi  bariroroani  ong  gen,  iandakgipa 
jatni  namnambegipa  dakmesokanirang,  golporang,  bewal  aro  dakbewalrang,  ring  ani  sulrang  aro 
aganrittingbaanirang  jatni  gisepo  gimajawa  ripinge  donangna  jotton  ka  paode  namgen.  Jatni  gisepo 
iandakgipa  namnambegipa  aganrittingbaanirang  da  o ia  chasong  dambe  ringsong  gitalo,  skie  aro 
ma  sie  ra  rikna  gisik  nanggijanio  gimaangengaha.  Iandakgipa  ringring  anirang,  chrok  mesaanirang, 
doka  damanirang  aro  plak  changa  sapanirangba  magitcham  pagitcham  chagitchamrangni  bonanga 
baksa  apsan  bon  angengaha,  uarangko  da  o an  ching  chol  ong  mittingon  skie  ra  rikna  aro  chasongni 
chasongna  ja  chong  jatni  gisepo  donangrikrikna,  Somoi  an  chingna  namen  gamchatbegipa  ong  enga. 
Atchu  Ambini,  Bitchi  Budeni  somoirang  re  angengaha,  am  oba  ja  mano  man  taijawaha.

MESOKANI  - I 
KALSINNI  PAGIPA  SIA 
Ka  sara  jikgipa  aro  dedrang.  Anga  nasimangko  wate  ream 
sal  sokaha.  Anga  ia  agilsako  rona  skugenchim  ongoba 
angna  somoi  dongjaha.  Uni  gimin  nasimang  name  tom'tome 
aro  kasagrike  dongbo.  Chi....  chi....  chi....  (sia) 
Ang"  Se  ratcha  maia  ong'  jok,  ku  rang  ku  sa  on  kubo, 
mikgron  mikkimitnabe,  nichaobo.  Dukan  matcholjawajok, 
saksan  birangrikainok,  jawa  chonnikrikainok.  Nangni

kasaa  dedrangko  wate  reangbebejok.  Bika  a dram  be  ainok, 
bean  dongainok,  sintie  dopilgmari  siainok.  Mikchi 
chiwaringari  angade,  akkal  do  budokari  chonainok. 
Dede  son  a nang'pade,  wate  kattangbebejok, 
Mikgron  mikchi  tipgija,  birangrikna  nangainok. 
Apa  na'ra  maia  ong'jok,  anga  nursijaha, 
Nang  ni  dongjagenchimode,  dukkan  chotchangjaha. 
Dede  sona  nang  padeni,  chingko  watangaijok, 
Mikgron  mikchi  ong'arisa,  an  ching  dongrikainok. 
Ama  na  a duk  man  oba,  chingko  chanchipabo, 
A'sak  dukko  man  genoba,  gisik  ka  dimebo.

changen  gita  nikamo  jong?

sandichengbo.  Sakoba  ka  a dongode  namjawa,  bobil 
onggrikgen.

Gipin  ong  ja  Sonatchisa,  bakbak  chakatbojok. 
Me  mang  ong  ja  agitalan,  na  mang  jajrengnabe, 
Sigiminan  tangchapil  e,  sokbajok  kennabe. 
Ama  na  a kenna  nangja,  ku  rang  knattimbo, 
Bebe  ra  na  amjaoba,  chakatba  e nibo. 
Mikgitalma,  jumangsama,  maia  ong  jok  da  alde, 
Maiban  appot  ong  taigenma,  Aman  bimangsake.

Darangnian  ku  rang  ong  ja,  Dede  sonatchinin, 
Bimang  chong  chong,  gipin  ong  ja  Ama  Sonatchian. 
Jeba  ong  bo  do  ga  oe,  anga  niatsrangen, 
Cha  u me  pu  ong  genoba,  angan  sisrangaigen. 
Simanaha  Ama  na  ara,  maikai  tangchapil  jok, 
Me  mangsama  putsama,  ang  de  ma  sijajok. 
Ai  sin  a amchakjacngjok,  bakbak  do  ga  obo, 
Bcbe  ra  na  amjaoba,  chakate  re  babo. 
Darang  gipin  ku'rang  ongja,  dede  sonatchian, 
Maia  a sel  ong  pilaba,  chakki  so  ebochang. 
(Kenne)  Aiao,  da  nang  gipin  ong  ja.  Sonatchi  sagian, 
7a*ma  rani  sokbapirjok,  an'chingni  rasongan. 
'Fa  rak  nokning  napbo  dede,  intang  jawa  nikgen, 
Re  baenba  ja  nengtakbo,  duk  suk  golpogen. 
Ta  ma  Rani  walni  somoi,  sawa  dilbapa  jok, 
Samtangtangchin  bobilrara,  na’ara  kenpajajok. 
jeba  ong  bo  sepangsrange,  ang  ba  re  e ninok.
